Model before rewriting:

find x: int(-3..-2, 0, 3)
find at_most_two: bool
find b: bool
find c: bool
find zero: bool
find mixed: int(-3..3)
find quotient: int(-3..3)
find remainder: int(0..1)

such that

mixed = product([x,toInt(b);int(1..)]),
(zero) <-> (product([x,toInt(b);int(1..)]) = 0),
product([x,toInt(and([b,x != 0;int(1..)]));int(1..)]) = mixed,
quotient = (x / sum([toInt(b),1;int(1..)])),
remainder = x % sum([toInt(b),1;int(1..)]),
(at_most_two) <-> (sum([toInt(b),toInt(c),toInt(x % 2 != 0),toInt(x != 0);int(1..4)]) <= 2)

mixed
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
mixed
new variables:
  find mixed#order_1: bool
  find mixed#order_2: bool
  find mixed#order_3: bool
  find mixed#order_4: bool
  find mixed#order_5: bool
  find mixed#order_6: bool
  find mixed#order_7: bool
new constraints:
  mixed#order_1
  or([!(mixed#order_2),mixed#order_1;int(1..)])
  or([!(mixed#order_3),mixed#order_2;int(1..)])
  or([!(mixed#order_4),mixed#order_3;int(1..)])
  or([!(mixed#order_5),mixed#order_4;int(1..)])
  or([!(mixed#order_6),mixed#order_5;int(1..)])
  or([!(mixed#order_7),mixed#order_6;int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Order, [mixed#order_1,mixed#order_2,mixed#order_3,mixed#order_4,mixed#order_5,mixed#order_6,mixed#order_7;int(1..)] [-3, 3]) >= -3,SATInt(Order, [mixed#order_1,mixed#order_2,mixed#order_3,mixed#order_4,mixed#order_5,mixed#order_6,mixed#order_7;int(1..)] [-3, 3]) <= 3;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

__0
new variables:

new SAT encoding decisions:
